<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-24ECA0A4-CA46-4E35-AE55-0CC8E4DC839A'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

Divide: $108 \div 4$

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 108 \div 4 \\\\
&= \dfrac {108} {4} \\\\
&= \dfrac {27 \times 4} {4} \\\\
&= \dfrac {27 \times \cancel {4}} {\cancel {4}} \\\\
&= 27
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$27$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
&= 108 \div 4 \\\\
&= \dfrac {108} {4} \\\\
&= \dfrac {27 \times 4} {4} \\\\
&= \dfrac {27 \times \cancel {4}} {\cancel {4}} \\\\
&= 27
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>

</div>

</code></pre>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-089B56B3-5FE5-42D9-A2A5-5A1136AECEE9'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

Find the cost of downloading $5$ apps if each one costs $\pounds 2.98$.

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Cost of downloading five apps}        &= \text{Cost of downloading one app} \times \text{Number of apps} \\\\
                                            &= 2.98 \times 5 \\\\
                                            &= \dfrac {298} {100} \times 5 \\\\
                                            &= \dfrac {298 \times 5} {100} \\\\
                                            &= \dfrac {1490} {100} \\\\
                                            &= \pounds 14.9
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$\pounds 14.9$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Cost of downloading five apps}        &= \text{Cost of downloading one app} \times \text{Number of apps} \\\\
                                            &= 2.98 \times 5 \\\\
                                            &= \dfrac {298} {100} \times 5 \\\\
                                            &= \dfrac {298 \times 5} {100} \\\\
                                            &= \dfrac {1490} {100} \\\\
                                            &= \pounds 14.9
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$\pounds 14.9$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-DEF306C6-4B02-40CC-97C6-0C1E0966876A'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

Divide $3$ by $0.5$

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 3 \div 0.5 \\\\
&= 3 \div \dfrac {5} {10} \\\\
&= 3 \times \dfrac {10} {5} \\\\
&= \dfrac {3 \times 10} {5} \\\\
&= \dfrac {3 \times 2 \times 5} {5} \\\\
&= \dfrac {3 \times 2 \times \cancel {5}} {\cancel {5}} \\\\
&= 3 \times 2 \\\\
&= 6
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$6$

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>

&= 3 \times \dfrac {10} {5} \\\\
&= \dfrac {3 \times 10} {5} \\\\
&= \dfrac {3 \times 2 \times 5} {5} \\\\
&= \dfrac {3 \times 2 \times \cancel {5}} {\cancel {5}} \\\\
&= 3 \times 2 \\\\
&= 6
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-17EBE603-FE66-4DF1-A566-229E356FE83D'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

Use the fact that $96815 \times 123 = 11908245$ to work out $96816  \times 123$

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
&= 96816 \times 123 \\\\
&= (96815 + 1) \times 123 \\\\
&= (96815 \times 123) + (1 \times 123) \\\\
&= 11908245 + 123 \\\\
&= 11908368
\end{aligned}
$

</div>

&= 11908368
\end{aligned}
$

{+} 2 &= 5 \green
{\times} 2 \\\\
10 &= 10
\end{aligned}
$

</div>

{\times} 3 &= 60 \green
{\div} 5 \\\\

\end{aligned}

<div class='question question'>

The three-digit number shown below can be divided by $3$ without a remainder. 

Fill in the box to show the largest possible value that the last digit could be.

$\boxed{5} \ \boxed{3} \ \boxed{ \phantom{5}}$

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

7

Lets verify,

For the number to be divisible by 3, the sum of digits has to be divisible by 3.

Therefore maximum possible sum $= 15$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

5

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>7
<emptyline>

<emptyline>

<emptyline>
Therefore maximum possible sum $= 15$
</code></pre>
</div>

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-7B74815D-732E-4E67-B878-86DB82735EBA'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

At a busy railway station trains leave from platform $5$ every $6 \ \text{minutes}$ and from platform $8$ every 
$10 \ \text{minutes}$. Trains leave from both platforms at $15{:}57$. When do trains next leave both platforms at the same time?

</div>
<div class='workings'>
<div class='working'>

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Time trains leave both platforms simultaneously }     &= 15{:}57 + 30 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
                                                            &= 16{:}27 
\end{aligned}
$

</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answer'>

$ 16{:}27 $

</div>
</div>
<div class='workingscodeblock'>
<div class='workingcodeblock'>

<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Time trains leave both platforms simultaneously }     &= 15{:}57 + 30 \ \text{minutes} \\\\
                                                            &= 16{:}27 
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>
<div class='answers'>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>%empty%</code></pre>
</div>
<div class='answercodeblock'>
<pre class='language-latex'><code class='language-latex'>$ 16{:}27 $
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<div class='question question'>

If it is a cold rainy day then I wear a raincoat. If it is a warm rainy day then I carry an umbrella. 
If I wear my raincoat then I also wear either gloves or a hat but not both. 
If I carry an umbrella I always wear gloves and sometimes wear my hat as well. 
I never carry an umbrella and wear my raincoat on the same day. 
For each of the following, state whether it is definitely true, false or uncertain.

A sunflower seed takes a week to germinate after it has been sown in the ground. After germination it grows the same number centimetres each day.

Maya sows a sunflower seed. She measures the height of the plant at midday on the $16 \text{th}$ 
August and again at midday on the $21 \text{st}$ August. These
two measurements are recorded as $48 \ \text{cm}$ and $68 \ \text{cm}$ respectively.

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-EFB9945E-41A3-4ED9-8B98-1E7688CB0B4D'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

The diagram shows the one-way cycle paths in a park.

Work out the total number of possible routes to go:

-  from $A$ to $C$
-  from $A$ to $D$
-  from $A$ to $E$

![missing image](/papers/missing_image.svg)

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-A7F0B23B-97E3-432A-B51B-2D02118F9825'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

A sequence of numbers which get multiplied (or divided) by a fixed amount each time is called a geometric progression $(GP)$. 
For example, the sequence:

$1 \text{st}$ term $= 6, \quad 2 \text{nd}$ term $= 12, \quad 3 \text{rd}$ term $= 24, \quad 4 \text{th}$ term $= 48$ 

is a $GP$ because you multiply by $2$ to go from one term to the next.

<p class='review_status initial' id='SUBTAG-STATUS-66E82DE8-5928-4039-A1D7-0B0410BA4486'>Initial</p>
<div class='question question'>

Snow White and the seven dwarves work in a mining company. 
As Chief Executive, Snow White earns as much as all the dwarves put together. 
Sneezy gets $24$ gold galleons a day. Sleepy earns $50\%$ more than Happy. 
Bashful earns twice as much as Grumpy who earns one more galleon than Sneezy. 
Doc gets $24$ galleons a day more than Dopey and between them they get four times that of Sneezy. 
Sleepy earns seven-eighths of Sneezy's salary.

Complete the table below to work out how much Snow White earns in a day.

![missing table](/papers/missing_table.svg)


Bashful Doc Dopey Grumpy Happy Sleepy Sneezy Snow White Gold Galleons Snow White
